| 62b7639a5a68:19 | How Germany prices and documents dealings between a business and its related parties abroad: the arm's length rule, the records a business must keep, the master file, the country-by-country report, what must be handed over in a tax audit and when, the surcharges, and advance pricing procedures. It is for German companies, partnerships and permanent establishments that deal with related parties abroad, and for their advisers. Figures are for tax year 2026. The statute figures are read from the consolidated federal law pages as they stood in September 2026. Four finance ministry papers are also used, each dated another year: the administrative principles on transfer pricing of 12 December 2024, the information sheet on the transaction matrix of 2 April 2025, the application decree on advance pricing procedures of 26 June 2024, and the administrative principles on documentation duties of 3 December 2020. The 2020 paper is older than the changes that apply from 1 January 2025. This Guide uses it only for points that the later law did not change, and names it each time. | Exact wording retained: Transfer pricing in Germany (Verrechnungspreise), currentLF19 |
| 62b7639a5a68:23 | Version 1.0 of this Guide, refreshed against the official pages for 2026. | Presentation version updated to1.2; currentLF23, no substantive rule dropped |
| 62b7639a5a68:27 | **Quick Reference** | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF27 |
| 62b7639a5a68:29 | \| Field \| Value \| | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF29 |
| 62b7639a5a68:31 | \| Country \| Germany (Federal Republic of Germany) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF31 |
| 62b7639a5a68:32 | \| Tax authority \| Local tax office (Finanzamt): assessment and tax audit. Federal Central Tax Office (Bundeszentralamt für Steuern, BZSt): receives country-by-country reports and runs advance pricing procedures \| | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF32 |
| 62b7639a5a68:33 | \| Key TP legislation \| § 1 Außensteuergesetz (AStG, Foreign Tax Act): the arm's length rule. § 90(3) and (4) Abgabenordnung (AO, General Tax Code): records and handover deadlines. § 162(3) and (4) AO: estimates and surcharges. § 138a AO: country-by-country report. § 89a AO: advance pricing procedures \| | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF33 |
| 62b7639a5a68:34 | \| Documentation regulation \| GAufzV (Gewinnabgrenzungsaufzeichnungs-Verordnung) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF34 |
| 62b7639a5a68:35 | \| Who is a related party \| A holding of at least one quarter, direct or indirect, in capital, membership rights, participation rights, voting rights or company assets; a claim to at least one quarter of the profit or of the liquidation proceeds; controlling influence, direct or indirect; a third person who stands in one of these positions to both sides; or influence that comes from outside the business relation, or an own interest in the other side earning the income (§ 1(2) AStG). The law prints the share in words \| | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF35 |
| 62b7639a5a68:36 | \| Which dealings are covered \| Business relations with a related party abroad, and dealings between a business and its permanent establishment in another state (§ 1(1), (4) and (5) AStG). A partnership is itself a taxpayer under this rule. Not covered: dealings that rest on an agreement under company law, meaning one that directly changes the legal position as a shareholder (§ 1(4) sentence 1 no. 1 b AStG) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF36 |
| 62b7639a5a68:37 | \| Direction of the rule \| § 1 AStG applies where income was reduced by terms that are not at arm's length. § 1(1) AStG names the case where income was reduced ("gemindert"). For permanent establishments § 1(5) AStG names two cases: the German income of a taxpayer with limited tax liability is reduced, or the foreign income of a taxpayer with unlimited tax liability is raised \| | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF37 |
| 62b7639a5a68:38 | \| OECD guidelines \| The ministry's 2024 administrative principles say the German tax administration in principle follows the OECD transfer pricing guidelines, within the domestic law in force, whether or not a tax treaty applies (no. 2.2) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF38 |
| 62b7639a5a68:39 | \| Effective date (current regime) \| Records regulation: financial years beginning after 31 December 2016 (§ 7 GAufzV). Transaction matrix, the 30-day handover and the current surcharge rule: from 1 January 2025 (Art. 97 § 37(5) EGAO). Current § 1 AStG: from assessment period 2022. The financing rules in § 1(3d) and (3e) AStG: from assessment period 2024 (§ 21 AStG) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF39 |
| 62b7639a5a68:40 | \| Currency \| EUR \| | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF40 |
| 62b7639a5a68:41 | \| Documentation language \| German. The tax office can allow another language on application (§ 2(5) GAufzV) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF41 |
| 62b7639a5a68:42 | \| Guide version \| 1.0 \| | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF42 |
| 62b7639a5a68:44 | The law pages behind this table: § 1 AStG at https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/astg/__1.html and § 90 AO at https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__90.html | Exact wording retained: Section 1: Quick Reference, currentLF44 |
| 62b7639a5a68:48 | - **Who must keep records.** A taxpayer must keep records on the kind and content of its business relations within § 1(4) AStG. See § 90(3) AO at https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__90.html The rule that the records serve, § 1 AStG, is about business relations abroad. The ministry describes the transaction matrix as a table of the taxpayer's cross-border business relations with related parties and permanent establishments (information sheet of 2 April 2025, part I). | Exact wording retained: Section 2: Documentation Requirements, currentLF48 |
| 62b7639a5a68:49 | - **No size limit for the duty itself.** § 90(3) AO prints no minimum amount. Smaller businesses get a lighter way to meet the duty, not a release from it. See 2.2. | Exact wording retained: Section 2: Documentation Requirements, currentLF49 |
| 62b7639a5a68:50 | - **Three parts.** The records cover (1) an overview of the transactions (Transaktionsmatrix), (2) a presentation of the transactions (Sachverhaltsdokumentation) and (3) the economic and legal basis for arm's length terms, with the time the price was set, the method used and the comparables used (Angemessenheitsdokumentation). | Exact wording retained: Section 2: Documentation Requirements, currentLF50 |
| 62b7639a5a68:51 | - **A fourth part for larger group companies.** The master file. See 2.1. | Exact wording retained: Section 2: Documentation Requirements, currentLF51 |
| 62b7639a5a68:52 | - **The standard.** The records must show a serious effort to deal at arm's length. An expert third party must be able to see, within a reasonable time, what the taxpayer did and whether it kept to the arm's length rule. See § 2(1) GAufzV at https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gaufzv_2017/__2.html Records that are unusable in essence count as not made. See § 1(1) GAufzV at https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gaufzv_2017/__1.html | Exact wording retained: Section 2: Documentation Requirements, currentLF52 |
| 62b7639a5a68:53 | - **One method is enough.** The taxpayer does not have to keep records for more than one suitable method (§ 2(2) GAufzV). | Exact wording retained: Section 2: Documentation Requirements, currentLF53 |
| 62b7639a5a68:54 | - **Per transaction, with grouping allowed.** Records are made per transaction. Transactions that are comparable in functions and risks may be grouped, if the grouping follows rules set in advance and the transactions are alike, or if grouping is usual between third parties. Group transfer pricing guidelines that are in fact followed can replace records per transaction (§ 2(3) GAufzV). | Exact wording retained: Section 2: Documentation Requirements, currentLF54 |
| 62b7639a5a68:55 | - **Long-running arrangements.** If circumstances change in a way that matters for the price, the taxpayer must go on collecting information after the deal is closed. This applies above all where tax losses show up that a third party would not have accepted (§ 2(4) GAufzV). | Exact wording retained: Section 2: Documentation Requirements, currentLF55 |
| 62b7639a5a68:56 | - **Language.** Records are made in German. The tax office can allow exceptions on application. The application can be made before the records are written. It must be made at the latest without delay after the tax office asks for the records (§ 2(5) GAufzV). The ministry's 2020 paper adds that another living language, English above all, can be allowed if the records can still be understood in reasonable time, that this applies to the master file in particular, and that translations asked for later must then be supplied in reasonable time (no. 34). | Exact wording retained: Section 2: Documentation Requirements, currentLF56 |
| 62b7639a5a68:57 | - **Database searches.** A taxpayer that uses databases must disclose the search strategy, the search criteria, the result and the further selection steps. The whole search must be open to checking at the time of the audit. See § 4(3) GAufzV at https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gaufzv_2017/__4.html | Exact wording retained: Section 2: Documentation Requirements, currentLF57 |
| 62b7639a5a68:61 | **Master File: the turnover test** | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF61 |
| 62b7639a5a68:63 | \| What \| Value \| Note \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF63 |
| 62b7639a5a68:65 | \| Source \| all figures below \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__90.html \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF65 |
| 62b7639a5a68:66 | \| A business that is part of a multinational group must also keep a master file, unless its own turnover in the previous financial year was less than \| EUR 100 million \| § 90(3) sentence 3 AO: "weniger als 100 Millionen Euro betragen". The law prints "Millionen" in words \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF66 |
| 62b7639a5a68:68 | **Master File** | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF68 |
| 62b7639a5a68:70 | \| Item \| Detail \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF70 |
| 62b7639a5a68:72 | \| Required? \| Only if both are true: the business is part of a multinational group, AND its own turnover in the previous financial year was at least the amount in the table above. The test is the turnover of the German business itself, not the consolidated revenue of the group \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF72 |
| 62b7639a5a68:73 | \| How turnover is measured \| The ministry's 2020 paper (no. 55): turnover as defined in § 277(1) HGB, not consolidated, at home and abroad, with third parties and with related parties. Internal dealings between the business and its own permanent establishment are left out. "Business" there means a business with trade income under § 15(1) sentence 1 no. 1 EStG. It does not matter how small the foreign share of the turnover is \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF73 |
| 62b7639a5a68:74 | \| What is a multinational group \| At least two related businesses resident in different states, or one business with at least one permanent establishment in another state (§ 90(3) sentence 4 AO) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF74 |
| 62b7639a5a68:75 | \| Purpose \| An overview of the group's worldwide business and of the system it uses to set transfer prices (§ 90(3) sentence 3 AO) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF75 |
| 62b7639a5a68:76 | \| Format \| The 18 items listed in the annex to § 5 GAufzV. See the list below. The ministry's 2020 paper calls the list final (no. 56) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF76 |
| 62b7639a5a68:77 | \| Who writes it \| The taxpayer may use records made by another company of the same group, and adds to them where the annex asks for more (§ 5(1) GAufzV). The effort should be reasonable (§ 5(2) GAufzV) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF77 |
| 62b7639a5a68:78 | \| Filing \| In a tax audit: without being asked, within 30 days of the announcement of the audit order (§ 90(4) sentence 3 AO) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF78 |
| 62b7639a5a68:79 | \| Language \| German. Another language only if the tax office allows it on application (§ 2(5) and § 5(1) GAufzV) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF79 |
| 62b7639a5a68:81 | **Master File: contents (annex to § 5 GAufzV)** | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF81 |
| 62b7639a5a68:83 | \| What \| Value \| Note \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF63 |
| 62b7639a5a68:85 | \| Source \| all figures below \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gaufzv_2017/anlage.html \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF85 |
| 62b7639a5a68:86 | \| Supply chains must be described for the five products or services with the highest revenue, and for every other product or service that makes up more than this share of the group's revenue \| 5% \| Annex no. 4: "auf die jeweils mehr als 5 Prozent der Umsatzerlöse der Unternehmensgruppe entfallen" \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF86 |
| 62b7639a5a68:88 | The 18 items of the annex, in short: | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF88 |
| 62b7639a5a68:90 | 1. Chart of the legal and ownership structure and of where the companies and permanent establishments are. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF90 |
| 62b7639a5a68:91 | 2. Overview of the main drivers of the group's total profit. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF91 |
| 62b7639a5a68:92 | 3. Supply chains of the five products or services with the highest revenue. A clear chart is enough. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF92 |
| 62b7639a5a68:93 | 4. Supply chains of every other product or service above the share in the table. A clear chart is enough. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF93 |
| 62b7639a5a68:94 | 5. List and short description of important service agreements inside the group (without research and development), with the capacities of the main service sites and the pricing policy for services. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF94 |
| 62b7639a5a68:95 | 6. The main geographic markets for the products and services in items 3 and 4. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF95 |
| 62b7639a5a68:96 | 7. Short functional analysis: the key functions, important risks and important assets of each group company. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF96 |
| 62b7639a5a68:97 | 8. Important restructurings, acquisitions and disposals during the financial year. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF97 |
| 62b7639a5a68:98 | 9. The group's overall strategy for intangibles, with the sites of the main research and development facilities and of research and development management. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF98 |
| 62b7639a5a68:99 | 10. List of the intangibles that matter for transfer pricing and of the companies that own them. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF99 |
| 62b7639a5a68:100 | 11. List of important agreements on intangibles, including cost sharing, main research service agreements and licence agreements. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF100 |
| 62b7639a5a68:101 | 12. The group's pricing policy for research and development and for intangibles. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF101 |
| 62b7639a5a68:102 | 13. Important transfers of rights in intangibles during the financial year, with the companies, states and payments. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF102 |
| 62b7639a5a68:103 | 14. How the group is financed, including important financing from third parties. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF103 |
| 62b7639a5a68:104 | 15. The group companies with a central financing, cash management or asset management function, the law they are organised under and their place of effective management. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF104 |
| 62b7639a5a68:105 | 16. The group's pricing strategy for financing inside the group. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF105 |
| 62b7639a5a68:106 | 17. The group's consolidated accounts for the financial year, if any were drawn up. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF106 |
| 62b7639a5a68:107 | 18. List and short description of the group's existing unilateral advance pricing agreements and of other advance rulings on the split of income between states. | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF107 |
| 62b7639a5a68:109 | Where the annex uses open terms, the business may judge for itself, provided it judges the same way in every country, by disclosed criteria, and from year to year (annex, last sentence). | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF109 |
| 62b7639a5a68:113 | **Local File** | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF113 |
| 62b7639a5a68:115 | \| Item \| Detail \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF70 |
| 62b7639a5a68:117 | \| Name in the regulation \| Landesspezifische, unternehmensbezogene Dokumentation (§ 4 GAufzV) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF117 |
| 62b7639a5a68:118 | \| Required? \| Yes, for every taxpayer with business relations within § 1(4) AStG, so far as the records matter for checking those relations. There is no amount below which the duty ends. Smaller businesses meet it in a lighter way. See the relief table below \| | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF118 |
| 62b7639a5a68:119 | \| Format \| Four blocks (§ 4(1) GAufzV): (1) general information on shareholdings, the business and the organisation; (2) the business relations: kind and size, the contracts behind them, and a list of the main intangibles; (3) function and risk analysis, with the value chain and the taxpayer's share in it; (4) transfer pricing analysis: when the price was set, the information used, the method, why it is suitable, the calculations, and the comparables used \| | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF119 |
| 62b7639a5a68:120 | \| Extra records where the case calls for them \| Offsetting arrangements; cost sharing contracts and how the key is applied; mutual agreement or arbitration procedures and foreign advance rulings that touch the taxpayer; price adjustments; the causes of losses and the steps taken, where the taxpayer shows a tax loss from business relations in more than three financial years in a row; research records around a change of functions (§ 4(2) GAufzV) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF120 |
| 62b7639a5a68:121 | \| Filing \| The tax office can ask for the records at any time. They are due within 30 days of the request. In justified single cases the period can be made longer (§ 90(4) AO). As a rule the records should be asked for only for a tax audit (§ 2(6) GAufzV) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF121 |
| 62b7639a5a68:123 | **Relief for smaller businesses (§ 6 GAufzV)** | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF123 |
| 62b7639a5a68:125 | \| What \| Value \| Note \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF63 |
| 62b7639a5a68:127 | \| Source \| all figures below \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gaufzv_2017/__6.html \| | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF127 |
| 62b7639a5a68:128 | \| Total payments for supplies of goods from business relations with related parties in the current financial year: not more than \| six million euros \| § 6(2) no. 1 GAufzV: "sechs Millionen Euro nicht übersteigt". The regulation prints this amount in words \| | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF128 |
| 62b7639a5a68:129 | \| Total payments for everything other than supplies of goods from business relations with related parties in the current financial year: not more than \| EUR 600,000 \| § 6(2) no. 2 GAufzV: "nicht mehr als 600 000 Euro beträgt" \| | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF129 |
| 62b7639a5a68:131 | - **Both limits must be kept.** The two tests are joined by "and". A business over either limit is not a smaller business. | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF131 |
| 62b7639a5a68:132 | - **What the relief is.** It is not an exemption. The record duties count as met so far as the information given to the tax office meets the standard of § 2(1) GAufzV and is backed by existing documents presented when the tax office asks. The handover deadlines still apply (§ 6(1) GAufzV). The regulation still cites the old sentence numbers of § 90(3) AO for these deadlines; since 1 January 2025 the deadlines sit in § 90(4) AO. The ministry's 2020 paper puts it this way: the duty to present records can also be met by giving information orally in time and presenting documents that already exist (no. 38). | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF132 |
| 62b7639a5a68:133 | - **One year of lag, both ways.** If the amounts are passed in a financial year, the relief ends from the following financial year. If a business that did not have the relief falls below the amounts in a financial year, it is treated as a smaller business in the following financial year (§ 6(2) GAufzV). | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF133 |
| 62b7639a5a68:134 | - **Added together.** Connected German businesses and German permanent establishments of related persons are added together for the test (§ 6(3) GAufzV). | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF134 |
| 62b7639a5a68:135 | - **What is counted.** The ministry's 2020 paper (no. 39): payments for supplies and services received from foreign related parties and provided to them. Goods can be any tangible asset and any intangible. Dealings with German related parties are left out. | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF135 |
| 62b7639a5a68:136 | - **Also covered.** The same relief applies to taxpayers whose income from business relations is not profit income (§ 6(1) GAufzV). | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF136 |
| 62b7639a5a68:137 | - **The pricing rule is untouched.** The arm's length rule applies below the limits as well. The estimate and surcharge rules also apply to a smaller business that breaches the duties it still has (ministry's 2020 paper, no. 88). | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF137 |
| 62b7639a5a68:138 | - **Transaction matrix.** The pages do not say whether a smaller business must still hand over a transaction matrix. § 6 GAufzV dates from 2017, before the matrix existed, and still cites old sentence numbers. § 162(4) sentence 1 AO prints no exception for smaller businesses. | Exact wording retained: 2.2 Local File (Landesspezifische Dokumentation), currentLF138 |
| 62b7639a5a68:142 | From 1 January 2025 (Fourth Bureaucracy Relief Act, BEG IV). The law's own word is Transaktionsmatrix. It is one part of the records on business relations (§ 90(3) sentence 2 no. 1 AO). | Exact wording retained: 2.3 Transaction Matrix (Aufzeichnungen über die Geschäftsbeziehungen), currentLF142 |
| 62b7639a5a68:144 | **Transaction Matrix** | Exact wording retained: 2.3 Transaction Matrix (Aufzeichnungen über die Geschäftsbeziehungen), currentLF144 |
| 62b7639a5a68:146 | \| Item \| Detail \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF70 |
| 62b7639a5a68:148 | \| Source for all rows below \| https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Internationales_Steuerrecht/Allgemeine_Informationen/2025-04-02-merkblatt-zur-transaktionsmatrix.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=3 \| | Exact wording retained: 2.3 Transaction Matrix (Aufzeichnungen über die Geschäftsbeziehungen), currentLF148 |
| 62b7639a5a68:149 | \| What it is \| A structured table of the taxpayer's cross-border business relations with related parties and permanent establishments. The ministry says it supports the risk-based choice of cases and audit fields \| | Exact wording retained: 2.3 Transaction Matrix (Aufzeichnungen über die Geschäftsbeziehungen), currentLF149 |
| 62b7639a5a68:150 | \| Content \| (a) subject and kind of the transactions; (b) the parties, marked as recipient or provider; (c) volume and payment in euro; (d) the contract behind the transaction, named only, not attached; (e) the transfer pricing method used; (f) the tax jurisdictions concerned; (g) whether a transaction is not under the regular tax rules of the jurisdiction concerned, for example a licence box \| | Exact wording retained: 2.3 Transaction Matrix (Aufzeichnungen über die Geschäftsbeziehungen), currentLF150 |
| 62b7639a5a68:151 | \| Grouping \| Transactions with one related party or permanent establishment in one jurisdiction that are comparable in functions and risks may be grouped and entered as a group (§ 2(3) GAufzV) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.3 Transaction Matrix (Aufzeichnungen über die Geschäftsbeziehungen), currentLF151 |
| 62b7639a5a68:152 | \| Submission \| In a tax audit: without being asked, within 30 days of the announcement of the audit order (§ 90(4) sentence 3 AO) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.3 Transaction Matrix (Aufzeichnungen über die Geschäftsbeziehungen), currentLF152 |
| 62b7639a5a68:153 | \| Format \| The information sheet of 2 April 2025 gives two sample layouts. The tax office can allow a different form, content or size. A taxpayer who wants that must say so and give reasons early, at the latest within the 30-day period \| | Exact wording retained: 2.3 Transaction Matrix (Aufzeichnungen über die Geschäftsbeziehungen), currentLF153 |
| 62b7639a5a68:154 | \| Earlier years \| The sheet's example year is 2025: an audit order issued in 2025 as a rule also covers years before 2025, and the matrix must then be made for those earlier years too. The 30-day period also applies to a request for the matrix made in 2025 where the audit order was issued before 1 January 2025 \| | Exact wording retained: 2.3 Transaction Matrix (Aufzeichnungen über die Geschäftsbeziehungen), currentLF154 |
| 62b7639a5a68:155 | \| Other audits \| Where the audit does not look at income tax matters with a foreign link (VAT special audits, wage tax audits, insurance tax audits), the matrix, the master file and the records on extraordinary transactions are due only on separate request \| | Exact wording retained: 2.3 Transaction Matrix (Aufzeichnungen über die Geschäftsbeziehungen), currentLF155 |
| 62b7639a5a68:156 | \| If it is not presented \| The fixed surcharge in the table in Section 6 \| | Exact wording retained: 2.3 Transaction Matrix (Aufzeichnungen über die Geschäftsbeziehungen), currentLF156 |
| 62b7639a5a68:160 | **CbCR: the revenue test** | Exact wording retained: 2.4 Country-by-Country Report (CbCR), currentLF160 |
| 62b7639a5a68:162 | \| What \| Value \| Note \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF63 |
| 62b7639a5a68:164 | \| Source \| all figures below \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__138a.html \| | Exact wording retained: 2.4 Country-by-Country Report (CbCR), currentLF164 |
| 62b7639a5a68:165 | \| A German group parent must file the report when the consolidated revenue shown in the group accounts for the previous financial year was at least \| EUR 750 million \| § 138a(1) no. 2 AO: "mindestens 750 Millionen Euro betragen". The law prints "Millionen" in words \| | Exact wording retained: 2.4 Country-by-Country Report (CbCR), currentLF165 |
| 62b7639a5a68:167 | **CbCR** | Exact wording retained: 2.4 Country-by-Country Report (CbCR), currentLF167 |
| 62b7639a5a68:169 | \| Item \| Detail \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF70 |
| 62b7639a5a68:171 | \| Who files \| A business with its seat or management in Germany that draws up consolidated accounts, or must do so under rules other than tax law (German group parent), if both are true: the group accounts include at least one foreign business or foreign permanent establishment, AND the revenue test in the table above is met (§ 138a(1) AO) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.4 Country-by-Country Report (CbCR), currentLF171 |
| 62b7639a5a68:172 | \| Who does not \| A German business that is itself included in the consolidated accounts of another business, unless it is appointed or the fallback rule applies (§ 138a(1) sentence 2 AO) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.4 Country-by-Country Report (CbCR), currentLF172 |
| 62b7639a5a68:173 | \| Appointed company \| A foreign group parent can appoint an included German group company to file for the group. That company then files with the BZSt (§ 138a(3) AO) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.4 Country-by-Country Report (CbCR), currentLF173 |
| 62b7639a5a68:174 | \| Fallback \| If the BZSt has not received the report of a group with a foreign parent, an included German group company must as a rule file it. When one German company files, the others are free. A company that cannot get or make the report must tell the BZSt within the filing period and give all the data it has or can get (§ 138a(4) AO). The same duties apply to the German permanent establishment of a foreign business that is included in consolidated accounts as foreign group parent or as included foreign group company (§ 138a(4) sentence 5 AO) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.4 Country-by-Country Report (CbCR), currentLF174 |
| 62b7639a5a68:175 | \| Filing deadline \| At the latest one year after the end of the financial year that the report covers (§ 138a(6) AO) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.4 Country-by-Country Report (CbCR), currentLF175 |
| 62b7639a5a68:176 | \| Filing method \| Electronic, in the officially prescribed data set. The BZSt takes the data as an XML file through its bulk data interface (DIP) or by file upload in the BZSt online.portal. See https://www.bzst.de/DE/Unternehmen/Intern_Informationsaustausch/CountryByCountryReporting/ElektronischeDatenuebermittlung/elektronische_Datenuebermittlung.html \| | Exact wording retained: 2.4 Country-by-Country Report (CbCR), currentLF176 |
| 62b7639a5a68:177 | \| Notification \| There is no separate yearly notice to the BZSt. A German business states in its tax return whether it is a German group parent, an appointed company, or an included German company of a group with a foreign parent. In the last case it also states which business files the report and with which authority. If that statement is missing, the included German company must itself file the report on time (§ 138a(5) AO). The same applies to such a German permanent establishment (§ 138a(5) sentence 4 AO). The BZSt's questions and answers page says the BZSt need not be told in advance which group company will file: https://www.bzst.de/DE/Unternehmen/Intern_Informationsaustausch/CountryByCountryReporting/FAQ/FAQ_node.html Its page on tax return statements says the corporate tax return takes these entries in Anlage WA: https://www.bzst.de/DE/Unternehmen/Intern_Informationsaustausch/CountryByCountryReporting/Steuererklaerungen/steuererklaerungen_node.html \| | Exact wording retained: 2.4 Country-by-Country Report (CbCR), currentLF177 |
| 62b7639a5a68:178 | \| Content \| Per tax jurisdiction: revenue with related and with unrelated businesses, income tax paid and accrued, profit before income tax, equity, retained earnings, number of employees, tangible assets. Plus a list of all businesses and permanent establishments with their main activities (§ 138a(2) AO) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.4 Country-by-Country Report (CbCR), currentLF178 |
| 62b7639a5a68:179 | \| Effective \| Financial years beginning after 31 December 2015. The fallback rule and the tax return statement: financial years beginning after 31 December 2016 (Art. 97 § 31 EGAO) \| | Exact wording retained: 2.4 Country-by-Country Report (CbCR), currentLF179 |
| 62b7639a5a68:185 | - **Arm's length principle.** Where a taxpayer's income from a business relation abroad with a related party is reduced because the terms, prices above all, differ from what independent third parties would have agreed in the same or similar circumstances, the income is set as it would have been under terms agreed between independent third parties. See § 1(1) AStG at https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/astg/__1.html | Exact wording retained: 3.1 Definition, currentLF185 |
| 62b7639a5a68:186 | - **What the third parties are assumed to know.** They know all essential circumstances of the business relation and act as prudent and diligent managers (§ 1(1) sentence 3 AStG). | Exact wording retained: 3.1 Definition, currentLF186 |
| 62b7639a5a68:187 | - **Facts first.** The actual circumstances of the transaction decide. The law asks for a function and risk analysis (who performs which functions, bears which risks, uses which assets) and a comparability analysis. The circumstances at the time the transaction was agreed count (§ 1(3) AStG). | Exact wording retained: 3.1 Definition, currentLF187 |
| 62b7639a5a68:188 | - **Stacking.** If the arm's length rule leads to a larger correction than the other rules, the larger correction is made on top of them (§ 1(1) sentence 4 AStG). As other rules the 2024 administrative principles name, for companies, the hidden profit distribution and the hidden contribution, and for sole traders and partnerships, contributions and withdrawals (no. 1.1). | Exact wording retained: 3.1 Definition, currentLF188 |
| 62b7639a5a68:189 | - **Hypothetical test.** Where no comparable values can be found, a hypothetical arm's length test is made from the view of both the provider and the recipient, using economically recognised valuation methods (§ 1(3) sentence 7 AStG). | Exact wording retained: 3.1 Definition, currentLF189 |
| 62b7639a5a68:190 | - **No agreement under the law of obligations.** Where a transaction rests on no such agreement, the law assumes that independent prudent and diligent managers would have made one, or would assert an existing legal position, unless the taxpayer shows credibly otherwise in the single case (§ 1(4) sentence 2 AStG). | Exact wording retained: 3.1 Definition, currentLF190 |
| 62b7639a5a68:194 | **Accepted Methods** | Exact wording retained: 3.2 Accepted Methods, currentLF194 |
| 62b7639a5a68:196 | \| Method \| Accepted \| | Exact wording retained: 3.2 Accepted Methods, currentLF196 |
| 62b7639a5a68:198 | \| Source for all rows below \| https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Internationales_Steuerrecht/Allgemeine_Informationen/2024-12-12-vwg-verrechnungspreise-2024.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=1 \| | Exact wording retained: 3.2 Accepted Methods, currentLF198 |
| 62b7639a5a68:199 | \| Comparable uncontrolled price method (Preisvergleichsmethode) \| Yes (no. 3.9 a) \| | Exact wording retained: 3.2 Accepted Methods, currentLF199 |
| 62b7639a5a68:200 | \| Resale price method (Wiederverkaufspreismethode) \| Yes (no. 3.9 b) \| | Exact wording retained: 3.2 Accepted Methods, currentLF200 |
| 62b7639a5a68:201 | \| Cost plus method (Kostenaufschlagsmethode) \| Yes (no. 3.9 c) \| | Exact wording retained: 3.2 Accepted Methods, currentLF201 |
| 62b7639a5a68:202 | \| Transactional net margin method (geschäftsvorfallbezogene Nettomargenmethode) \| Yes (no. 3.9 d) \| | Exact wording retained: 3.2 Accepted Methods, currentLF202 |
| 62b7639a5a68:203 | \| Transactional profit split method (geschäftsvorfallbezogene Gewinnaufteilungsmethode) \| Yes (no. 3.9 e) \| | Exact wording retained: 3.2 Accepted Methods, currentLF203 |
| 62b7639a5a68:204 | \| Hypothetical arm's length test \| Yes, where no comparable values can be found (§ 1(3) sentence 7 AStG). The ministry expects it as a rule for intangibles and rights, for a relocation of functions, and for a profit split without comparable values (no. 3.12). Recognised methods are above all income value and discounted cash flow methods (no. 3.13) \| | Exact wording retained: 3.2 Accepted Methods, currentLF204 |
| 62b7639a5a68:206 | The ministry says the list is not closed and that a mix of methods can be right in a single case (no. 3.10). | Exact wording retained: 3.2 Accepted Methods, currentLF206 |
| 62b7639a5a68:210 | - **Preferred method.** The law has no ranking with one method on top. The arm's length price is set by the method that fits best, given the comparability analysis and the comparable values that are available (§ 1(3) sentence 5 AStG). Differences between the compared transactions and the tested one are removed by proper adjustments where that is possible and makes the comparison better (§ 1(3) sentence 6 AStG). The hypothetical arm's length test applies only when no comparable values can be found. | Exact wording retained: 3.3 Preferred Method, currentLF210 |
| 62b7639a5a68:214 | - **A range is normal.** Applying the arm's length rule as a rule gives a range of values (§ 1(3a) sentence 1 AStG). | Exact wording retained: 3.4 Range/Median, currentLF214 |
| 62b7639a5a68:215 | - **Narrowing.** The range is narrowed where differences in comparability remain. If the values themselves give no clue how to narrow, the quarter of the lowest values and the quarter of the highest values are left out (§ 1(3a) sentences 2 and 3 AStG). The ministry calls this the interquartile method (no. 3.30 of the 2024 administrative principles). | Exact wording retained: 3.4 Range/Median, currentLF215 |
| 62b7639a5a68:216 | - **Range/median adjustment.** If the value the taxpayer used lies outside the range, or outside the narrowed range, the median counts, unless the taxpayer shows credibly that another value inside the range fits the arm's length rule better (§ 1(3a) sentence 4 AStG). This is what the law prescribes, not a choice of the tax office. | Exact wording retained: 3.4 Range/Median, currentLF216 |
| 62b7639a5a68:217 | - **Hypothetical test.** The lowest price of the provider and the highest price of the recipient as a rule give an agreement range. The mean of that range is used, unless the taxpayer shows credibly that another value in it is at arm's length (§ 1(3a) sentences 5 and 6 AStG). | Exact wording retained: 3.4 Range/Median, currentLF217 |
| 62b7639a5a68:218 | - **No records, worse range.** Where records are missing or unusable and the tax office must estimate, a range can be used in full against the taxpayer. See Section 6. | Exact wording retained: 3.4 Range/Median, currentLF218 |
| 62b7639a5a68:222 | **Filing Obligations** | Exact wording retained: Section 4: Filing Obligations, currentLF222 |
| 62b7639a5a68:224 | \| Obligation \| Detail \| Law and link \| | Exact wording retained: Section 4: Filing Obligations, currentLF224 |
| 62b7639a5a68:226 | \| Transaction matrix \| Hand over within 30 days of the announcement of the audit order, without being asked \| § 90(4) sentence 3 AO, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__90.html \| | Exact wording retained: Section 4: Filing Obligations, currentLF226 |
| 62b7639a5a68:227 | \| Master File \| Same, where the turnover test in 2.1 is met \| § 90(4) sentence 3 AO, same page \| | Exact wording retained: Section 4: Filing Obligations, currentLF227 |
| 62b7639a5a68:228 | \| Extraordinary transactions \| Same. The records themselves must be made close in time to the transaction. See Section 5 \| § 90(3) sentence 5 and (4) sentence 3 AO, same page \| | Exact wording retained: Section 4: Filing Obligations, currentLF228 |
| 62b7639a5a68:229 | \| Local File \| Hand over within 30 days of a request. The tax office can make the request at any time, also during the audit and also outside an audit \| § 90(4) sentences 1 to 3 AO, same page \| | Exact wording retained: Section 4: Filing Obligations, currentLF229 |
| 62b7639a5a68:230 | \| Additions \| The records must be added to when the tax office asks \| § 90(3) sentence 6 AO, same page \| | Exact wording retained: Section 4: Filing Obligations, currentLF230 |
| 62b7639a5a68:231 | \| CbCR \| Yearly electronic filing with the BZSt, where 2.4 applies \| § 138a(6) AO, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__138a.html \| | Exact wording retained: Section 4: Filing Obligations, currentLF231 |
| 62b7639a5a68:232 | \| CbCR notification \| No separate notice to the BZSt. The statement is made in the tax return \| § 138a(5) AO, same page \| | Exact wording retained: Section 4: Filing Obligations, currentLF232 |
| 62b7639a5a68:233 | \| Corporate tax return \| Carries the CbCR statement (Anlage WA). Transfer pricing records are not filed with the return. They are handed over as the rows above say \| https://www.bzst.de/DE/Unternehmen/Intern_Informationsaustausch/CountryByCountryReporting/Steuererklaerungen/steuererklaerungen_node.html \| | Exact wording retained: Section 4: Filing Obligations, currentLF233 |
| 62b7639a5a68:237 | **Deadlines** | Exact wording retained: Section 5: Deadlines, currentLF237 |
| 62b7639a5a68:239 | \| Item \| Deadline \| Law and link \| | Exact wording retained: Section 5: Deadlines, currentLF239 |
| 62b7639a5a68:241 | \| Documentation preparation \| The law sets a time for making the records only for extraordinary transactions: close in time to the transaction. Records made within six months after the end of the financial year in which the transaction took place still count as made in time. All other records must be ready to hand over within the 30-day periods below \| § 90(3) sentence 5 AO and § 3(1) GAufzV, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gaufzv_2017/__3.html \| | Exact wording retained: Section 5: Deadlines, currentLF241 |
| 62b7639a5a68:242 | \| What is extraordinary \| Above all: making or changing long-term contracts with a large effect on income from business relations; asset transfers in a restructuring; transfer or letting of assets with a major change of functions and risks; transactions tied to a change of business strategy that matters for pricing; making cost sharing agreements \| § 3(2) GAufzV, same page \| | Exact wording retained: Section 5: Deadlines, currentLF242 |
| 62b7639a5a68:243 | \| Transaction matrix + Master File submission \| 30 days from the announcement of the audit order, without being asked. In force from 1 January 2025 \| § 90(4) sentence 3 AO and Art. 97 § 37(5) EGAO, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/aoeg_1977/art_97__37.html \| | Exact wording retained: Section 5: Deadlines, currentLF243 |
| 62b7639a5a68:244 | \| Extraordinary transactions \| 30 days from the announcement of the audit order, without being asked \| § 90(4) sentence 3 AO, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__90.html \| | Exact wording retained: Section 5: Deadlines, currentLF244 |
| 62b7639a5a68:245 | \| Local File submission \| 30 days from the request \| § 90(4) sentence 2 AO, same page \| | Exact wording retained: Section 5: Deadlines, currentLF245 |
| 62b7639a5a68:246 | \| Longer period \| Possible in justified single cases \| § 90(4) sentence 4 AO, same page \| | Exact wording retained: Section 5: Deadlines, currentLF246 |
| 62b7639a5a68:247 | \| CbCR filing \| At the latest one year after the end of the financial year \| § 138a(6) AO, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__138a.html \| | Exact wording retained: Section 5: Deadlines, currentLF247 |
| 62b7639a5a68:248 | \| Corporate tax return \| Seven months after the end of the calendar year. Where a tax adviser prepares the return: the last day of February of the second calendar year after the tax period. The tax office can call for it earlier in listed cases \| § 149(2) to (4) AO, https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__149.html \| | Exact wording retained: Section 5: Deadlines, currentLF248 |
| 62b7639a5a68:252 | **Surcharges for transfer pricing records (§ 162(4) AO)** | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF252 |
| 62b7639a5a68:254 | \| What \| Value \| Note \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF63 |
| 62b7639a5a68:256 | \| Source \| all figures below \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__162.html \| | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF256 |
| 62b7639a5a68:257 | \| Fixed surcharge where the taxpayer presents no records on a transaction, or the records presented on a transaction are unusable in essence, or the transaction matrix is not presented \| EUR 5,000 \| § 162(4) sentence 1 AO: "ist ein Zuschlag von 5 000 Euro festzusetzen" \| | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF257 |
| 62b7639a5a68:258 | \| Where it comes to more than the fixed surcharge: lowest share of the extra income that results from a correction under § 162(3) AO \| 5% \| § 162(4) sentence 2 AO: "Der Zuschlag beträgt mindestens 5 Prozent und höchstens 10 Prozent des Mehrbetrags der Einkünfte" \| | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF258 |
| 62b7639a5a68:259 | \| Highest share of that extra income \| 10% \| Same sentence: "höchstens 10 Prozent des Mehrbetrags der Einkünfte" \| | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF259 |
| 62b7639a5a68:260 | \| Usable records presented late: highest surcharge \| EUR 1,000,000 \| § 162(4) sentence 4 AO: "beträgt der Zuschlag bis zu 1 000 000 Euro" \| | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF260 |
| 62b7639a5a68:261 | \| Usable records presented late: lowest surcharge for each full day after the deadline \| EUR 100 \| Same sentence: "mindestens jedoch 100 Euro für jeden vollen Tag der Fristüberschreitung" \| | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF261 |
| 62b7639a5a68:263 | - **Fixed amount or share.** The fixed surcharge is the floor. The share of the extra income applies only when it comes to more than the fixed surcharge. | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF263 |
| 62b7639a5a68:264 | - **Late is a different case from missing.** The late surcharge is for usable records that arrive after the deadline. It can be set in part amounts for full weeks and months of delay. | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF264 |
| 62b7639a5a68:265 | - **When it is set.** As a rule after the tax audit has ended. | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF265 |
| 62b7639a5a68:266 | - **How the amount is chosen.** Where the tax office has a choice, it weighs the purpose of the surcharge, the advantages the taxpayer drew and, for late records, the length of the delay. | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF266 |
| 62b7639a5a68:267 | - **When there is none.** No surcharge is set where the failure appears excusable or the fault is only slight. The fault of a legal representative or of a person who carries out the duty counts as the taxpayer's own. | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF267 |
| 62b7639a5a68:268 | - **In force.** The current § 162(4) AO applies from 1 January 2025. See Art. 97 § 37(5) EGAO at https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/aoeg_1977/art_97__37.html | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF268 |
| 62b7639a5a68:269 | - **Not deductible.** The ministry's 2020 paper treats the surcharge as an ancillary tax charge that cannot be deducted, and says it rules out neither a late filing surcharge nor fine or criminal proceedings (no. 89). | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF269 |
| 62b7639a5a68:271 | **Estimates (§ 162(3) AO)** | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF271 |
| 62b7639a5a68:273 | - **Adverse estimation.** If the taxpayer presents no records on a transaction, or records that are unusable in essence, or did not make the records on an extraordinary transaction in time, the law presumes that the German income the records serve is higher than declared. The taxpayer can rebut this. | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF273 |
| 62b7639a5a68:274 | - **Burden of proof.** The law does not use these words. It works through that rebuttable presumption. If the tax office must then estimate and the income can only be fixed within a range, price ranges above all, the range can be used in full against the taxpayer. | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF274 |
| 62b7639a5a68:275 | - **Foreign related party stays silent.** Where usable records were presented, but there are signs that the income would be higher under the arm's length rule, and the doubts cannot be cleared up because a foreign related person does not meet its duties to cooperate or to give information, the range rule applies: the range can be used in full against the taxpayer (§ 162(3) sentence 3 AO). The law does not name the presumption for this case. | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF275 |
| 62b7639a5a68:276 | - **Which years.** § 162(3) AO in the version in force on 1 January 2023 applies first to taxes that arise after 31 December 2024. It also applies to earlier taxes where the audit order for them was announced after 31 December 2024. For other earlier taxes the version in force on 31 December 2022 goes on applying (Art. 97 § 37(2) and (3) EGAO). | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF276 |
| 62b7639a5a68:278 | **Fine for the country-by-country report (§ 379 AO)** | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF278 |
| 62b7639a5a68:280 | \| What \| Value \| Note \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF63 |
| 62b7639a5a68:282 | \| Source \| all figures below \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__379.html \| | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF282 |
| 62b7639a5a68:283 | \| Highest fine for not sending the country-by-country report, not sending it in full or not in time, and for the same failures with the notice under § 138a(4) sentence 3 AO \| EUR 10,000 \| § 379(5) AO: "mit einer Geldbuße bis zu 10 000 Euro geahndet werden" \| | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF283 |
| 62b7639a5a68:285 | - **Only with intent or recklessness** (leichtfertig), and only where the act cannot be punished under § 378 AO (§ 379(2) no. 1c and (5) AO). | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF285 |
| 62b7639a5a68:286 | - **Tax return statement.** The statement under § 138a(5) AO is not named in § 379(2) no. 1c AO. Its own consequence is in 2.4: without it, the included German company must file the report itself. | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF286 |
| 62b7639a5a68:287 | - **Who runs the fine procedure.** The competent tax authority of the federal state, not the BZSt. See https://www.bzst.de/DE/Unternehmen/Intern_Informationsaustausch/CountryByCountryReporting/FAQ/FAQ_node.html | Exact wording retained: Section 6: Penalties, currentLF287 |
| 62b7639a5a68:291 | **APA** | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF291 |
| 62b7639a5a68:293 | \| Item \| Detail \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF70 |
| 62b7639a5a68:295 | \| Source for all rows below \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__89a.html \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF295 |
| 62b7639a5a68:296 | \| Availability \| Yes, as a procedure between states (Vorabverständigungsverfahren). It needs a tax treaty with a mutual agreement procedure, a risk of double taxation for the facts, and a likely outcome: that double taxation is avoided and that both authorities read the treaty the same way (§ 89a(1) AO) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF296 |
| 62b7639a5a68:297 | \| Types \| Bilateral (Germany and one other state) or multilateral (several other states). § 89a AO describes a procedure between states only. The ministry's decree of 26 June 2024 says that unilateral binding rulings should not be given on cross-border profit allocation or on the allocation of profit to permanent establishments (no. 1.5) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF297 |
| 62b7639a5a68:298 | \| Governing authority \| BZSt, acting in agreement with the highest tax authority of the federal state concerned or the authority it appoints \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF298 |
| 62b7639a5a68:299 | \| Who can apply \| A person entitled to treaty benefits. The BZSt names a business resident in Germany, or a business with a German permanent establishment that is resident in a treaty state. As a rule the foreign side should apply to its own authority at the same time. A partnership as a rule cannot apply itself, unless it has opted for corporate taxation. Where it has not opted, as a rule each partner who is resident in one of the treaty states is the person entitled to treaty benefits (decree, no. 1.8). Several persons whose case can only be judged in one way must apply together (§ 89a(1) AO) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF299 |
| 62b7639a5a68:300 | \| Facts covered \| Precisely defined facts that are not yet realised when the application is made \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF300 |
| 62b7639a5a68:301 | \| Typical duration \| The term should as a rule not be longer than five years. It can be extended on application. On application the agreement can also be applied to assessment periods before the term (roll back), within the treaty's time limits for mutual agreement procedures. Both the extension and the roll back need the consent of the state-level authority and of the other state's authority (§ 89a(1) and (6) AO) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF301 |
| 62b7639a5a68:302 | \| Before applying \| A non-binding prefiling meeting can be suggested to the BZSt informally (decree, no. 1.18) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF302 |
| 62b7639a5a68:303 | \| Conditions \| Germany signs the agreement with the other state only if it stands at least under the condition that the applicant agrees to its content and waives legal remedies against tax assessments so far as they carry out the agreement correctly for the term. After signing, the BZSt tells the applicant the content and sets a period. If the applicant does not meet the conditions in time, the procedure fails (§ 89a(3) AO) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF303 |
| 62b7639a5a68:304 | \| Binding effect \| The local tax office is bound, unless the conditions in the agreement are not met or no longer met, the other state does not keep to the agreement, or the law it rests on is repealed or changed (§ 89a(4) AO) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF304 |
| 62b7639a5a68:305 | \| Annual reporting \| As a rule the agreement obliges the taxpayer to make and present a yearly report ("Compliance Report"). It shows that the facts were realised and the conditions kept, and points out every deviation. It goes to the BZSt and the local tax office at the same time, in German or with a German translation (decree, no. 4.2) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF305 |
| 62b7639a5a68:306 | \| Processing time \| The pages read for this Guide print no processing time \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF306 |
| 62b7639a5a68:308 | **Fees for an advance pricing procedure (§ 89a(7) AO)** | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF308 |
| 62b7639a5a68:310 | \| What \| Value \| Note \| | Exact wording retained: 2.1 Master File (Stammdokumentation), currentLF63 |
| 62b7639a5a68:312 | \| Source \| all figures below \| https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__89a.html \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF312 |
| 62b7639a5a68:313 | \| Fee for each application in a transfer pricing case \| EUR 30,000 \| "Die Gebühr beträgt 30 000 Euro für jeden Antrag im Sinne des Absatzes 1" \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF313 |
| 62b7639a5a68:314 | \| Fee for each application to extend an agreement \| EUR 15,000 \| "sowie 15 000 Euro für jeden Verlängerungsantrag nach Absatz 6 Satz 1" \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF314 |
| 62b7639a5a68:315 | \| Cut in the fee where a coordinated bilateral or multilateral tax audit has already been carried out on the facts and ended with agreed facts and an agreed tax treatment \| 75% \| "wird die Gebühr um 75 Prozent reduziert" \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF315 |
| 62b7639a5a68:316 | \| Smaller transfer pricing case, each application: the total of the transactions covered is not expected to pass the two amounts in the relief table in 2.2 \| EUR 10,000 \| "beträgt die Gebühr 10 000 Euro für jeden Antrag im Sinne des Absatzes 1" \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF316 |
| 62b7639a5a68:317 | \| Smaller transfer pricing case, each application to extend \| EUR 7,500 \| "7 500 Euro für jeden Antrag nach Absatz 6 Satz 1" \| | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF317 |
| 62b7639a5a68:319 | - **Cases that are not transfer pricing cases** pay one quarter of the standard fees. The law prints this share in words. | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF319 |
| 62b7639a5a68:320 | - **When the fee is due.** The fee is set before the procedure opens and is due within one month of the fee notice. The procedure opens only when the fee notice is final and the fee is paid (§ 89a(7) AO). | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF320 |
| 62b7639a5a68:321 | - **One fee or several.** Several applicants who must apply together pay one fee. One combined application that covers several treaty states pays a separate fee for each procedure (§ 89a(7) AO). | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF321 |
| 62b7639a5a68:322 | - **No refund.** A fee that is final is not paid back if the application is withdrawn or refused, or if the procedure fails (§ 89a(8) AO). | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF322 |
| 62b7639a5a68:323 | - **Same number, other rule.** The smaller-case fee in this table and the highest fine in the last table of Section 6 happen to be the same amount. They are different rules. | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF323 |
| 62b7639a5a68:325 | The ministry's decree of 26 June 2024: https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Weitere_Steuerthemen/Abgabenordnung/AO-Anwendungserlass/2024-06-26-aenderung-aeao-89-89a.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=6 The BZSt's service page: https://online.portal.bzst.de/SharedDocs/Leistungsbeschreibung/DE/vorabverstaendigungsverfahren_in_verrechnungspreisfaellen.html | Exact wording retained: Section 7: Advance Pricing Agreements (APA), currentLF325 |
| 62b7639a5a68:329 | The pages read for this Guide print no general safe harbour. § 1 AStG prints no minimum amount. What the pages do print: | Exact wording retained: Section 8: Safe Harbours, currentLF329 |
| 62b7639a5a68:331 | **Safe Harbours** | Exact wording retained: Section 8: Safe Harbours, currentLF331 |
| 62b7639a5a68:333 | \| Area \| Detail \| | Exact wording retained: Section 8: Safe Harbours, currentLF333 |
| 62b7639a5a68:335 | \| Source for all rows below \| https://www.bundesfinanzministerium.de/Content/DE/Downloads/BMF_Schreiben/Internationales_Steuerrecht/Allgemeine_Informationen/2024-12-12-vwg-verrechnungspreise-2024.pdf?__blob=publicationFile&v=1 \| | Exact wording retained: 3.2 Accepted Methods, currentLF198 |
| 62b7639a5a68:336 | \| Low-value intra-group services \| The price is as a rule set by the cost plus method. A cost mark-up of five percent (the paper prints it in words) can as a rule be seen as arm's length, if the group can show that it applies it uniformly in practice (no. 3.74). Covered: supporting services that are not the group's main business with third parties, use or create no unique and valuable intangibles, and carry no significant risk. Examples: bookkeeping, preparing tax returns, staff recruitment (no. 3.75 and 3.76). Never covered: research and development, manufacturing and production, sales, marketing and distribution (no. 3.77) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 8: Safe Harbours, currentLF336 |
| 62b7639a5a68:337 | \| Interest rates \| No safe harbour rate. Under § 1(3d) AStG interest paid to a related party abroad is not at arm's length so far as the rate is above the rate at which the business could borrow from third parties on the rating of the whole group, unless it is proved in the single case that a rating derived from the group rating is at arm's length. The expense is also not at arm's length if the taxpayer cannot show credibly that it could have serviced the debt for the whole term from the start, and that it needed the money and used it for the purpose of the business. The ministry adds: the group's credit standing counts, unless the borrower's own is better (no. 3.133). Where the group has no rating, the rating of its top company can be used. Where that company has none either, the ministry accepts, for simplicity, a group rating worked out as at the loan date from the group's financing costs with third parties. It also accepts a credit analysis that the Deutsche Bundesbank made for the group as at the contract date (no. 3.136) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 8: Safe Harbours, currentLF337 |
| 62b7639a5a68:338 | \| Passing on finance \| Arranging or passing on a financing relation inside a multinational group, and treasury tasks such as liquidity, financial risk or currency risk management, are as a rule services with few functions and low risk, unless a function and risk analysis proves otherwise (§ 1(3e) AStG) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 8: Safe Harbours, currentLF338 |
| 62b7639a5a68:339 | \| Cash pool leader \| As a rule a service with few functions and low risk, paid on a cost basis. The ministry does not object to a mark-up on the directly attributable costs between five percent and ten percent (printed in words). Financing costs are not part of the cost base (no. 3.152) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 8: Safe Harbours, currentLF339 |
| 62b7639a5a68:340 | \| Baseline distribution (Amount B) \| For transactions within chapter 3 of annex 4 of the paper, the ministry does not object if the price follows the simplified approach described there. Only for a business relation with a jurisdiction within annex 5 of the paper, with which Germany has a tax treaty and which is not a non-cooperative jurisdiction under the Steueroasen-Abwehrgesetz (no. 3.63a). First for assessment period 2025 (no. 6.2) \| | Exact wording retained: Section 8: Safe Harbours, currentLF340 |
| 62b7639a5a68:341 | \| Documentation thresholds \| Within both amounts in the relief table in 2.2 (neither is passed) the records duty is met in a lighter way. It does not fall away, and arm's length pricing must still be shown \| | Exact wording retained: Section 8: Safe Harbours, currentLF341 |
| 62b7639a5a68:342 | \| Small transactions \| No statutory minimum. Every business relation abroad with a related party is under the arm's length rule \| | Exact wording retained: Section 8: Safe Harbours, currentLF342 |
| 62b7639a5a68:344 | - **Start date of the financing rules.** § 1(3d) and (3e) AStG apply first for assessment period 2024. § 1(3d) does not apply to expenses that arise up to 31 December 2024 from financing relations that were agreed under civil law before 1 January 2024 and whose actual performance began before 1 January 2024. If such a relation was changed in a major way after 31 December 2023 and before 1 January 2025, § 1(3d) does not apply to expenses that arose before the change. See § 21(1a) AStG at https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/astg/__21.html | Exact wording retained: Section 8: Safe Harbours, currentLF344 |
| 62b7639a5a68:348 | Germany has no formal safe harbour. The documentation thresholds lower the paperwork, not the pricing standard: | Exact wording retained: 8.1 Practical Implications of Documentation Thresholds, currentLF348 |
| 62b7639a5a68:349 | - Within both amounts in the relief table in 2.2: no full Local File has to be written. The business answers the tax office's questions to the standard of § 2(1) GAufzV and backs the answers with the documents it already has, within the same 30-day periods. | Exact wording retained: 8.1 Practical Implications of Documentation Thresholds, currentLF349 |
| 62b7639a5a68:350 | - Own turnover in the previous financial year below the amount in the table in 2.1, or no multinational group: no Master File required. Group revenue is not the test. | Exact wording retained: 8.1 Practical Implications of Documentation Thresholds, currentLF350 |
| 62b7639a5a68:351 | - However, the general obligation under § 90 AO to cooperate with the tax office remains. For facts abroad the taxpayer must clear up the facts and get the evidence, and cannot plead that this is impossible if it could have secured the means when it set up its affairs (§ 90(2) AO). | Exact wording retained: 8.1 Practical Implications of Documentation Thresholds, currentLF351 |
| 62b7639a5a68:352 | - The tax office can still challenge pricing even without formal documentation requirements. | Exact wording retained: 8.1 Practical Implications of Documentation Thresholds, currentLF352 |

| 62b7639a5a68:383 | 1. Find the covered dealings. List every business relation with a related person abroad within § 1(2) and (4) AStG, and every dealing between the business and a permanent establishment in another state within § 1(5) AStG. § 1 AStG is about business relations abroad. Dealings between two German related parties follow other rules (see the refer list). https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/astg/__1.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF383 |
| 62b7639a5a68:384 | 2. Set or test each price under § 1(3) AStG: function and risk analysis, comparability analysis, then the method that fits best, on the facts at the time the transaction was agreed. Where no comparable values exist, use the hypothetical arm's length test. Check the result against the range and median rules of § 1(3a) AStG. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/astg/__1.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF384 |
| 62b7639a5a68:385 | 3. Test the relief for smaller businesses in § 6 GAufzV: both amounts in the relief table in 2.2, for the current financial year, with connected German businesses added. Remember the one-year lag. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gaufzv_2017/__6.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF385 |
| 62b7639a5a68:386 | 4. Build the records that § 90(3) AO asks for: the transaction matrix, the facts and the appropriateness analysis, with the content of § 4 GAufzV. Write them in German, or apply early for another language under § 2(5) GAufzV. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gaufzv_2017/__4.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF386 |
| 62b7639a5a68:387 | 5. Test the master file duty under § 90(3) sentence 3 AO: part of a multinational group, and own turnover of the previous financial year against the table in 2.1. If it applies, prepare the items of the annex to § 5 GAufzV. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gaufzv_2017/__5.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF387 |
| 62b7639a5a68:388 | 6. Record extraordinary transactions close in time, at the latest within six months after the end of the financial year, under § 3 GAufzV. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/gaufzv_2017/__3.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF388 |
| 62b7639a5a68:389 | 7. When an audit order is announced, note the date. Under § 90(4) AO the transaction matrix, the master file and the records on extraordinary transactions are due 30 days later without a request. Everything else is due 30 days after a request. Ask for a longer period early and give reasons. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__90.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF389 |
| 62b7639a5a68:390 | 8. For groups at the revenue test in 2.4: file the country-by-country report with the BZSt under § 138a AO within one year after the financial year ends, and make the group statement in the tax return (Anlage WA for corporations). https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__138a.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF390 |
| 62b7639a5a68:391 | 9. Where certainty is wanted in advance and a tax treaty applies, consider an advance pricing procedure under § 89a AO with the BZSt. Weigh the fee in Section 7 and the waiver of legal remedies. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__89a.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF391 |
| 62b7639a5a68:392 | 10. If records were missed or are late, read § 162(3) and (4) AO before answering the tax office: late usable records and missing records lead to different surcharges, and an excusable failure leads to none. https://www.gesetze-im-internet.de/ao_1977/__162.html | Exact wording retained: The method, step by step, currentLF392 |
| 62b7639a5a68:396 | - Who are the related parties abroad: holdings of at least one quarter, control, or a shared parent? Is there a permanent establishment abroad, or is the client the German permanent establishment of a foreign business? | Exact wording retained: Ask the client first, currentLF434 |
| 62b7639a5a68:397 | - What are the total payments in the current financial year with foreign related parties, split into supplies of goods and everything else (services, interest, licences)? Which connected German businesses must be added? | Exact wording retained: Ask the client first, currentLF435 |

## Writer checks and limits

The 124 original prepared anchors plus narrow date/effect additions are exact substrings of pinned readers. No claim that5434lines of2024BMFannexes or1003lines of2020principles were wholly read. Selected named paragraphs only; actual-scope log and bank quote coordinates use literalLF, including PDF formfeeds as ordinary characters. Older2020paragraphs are used only with the unchanged own current statute/regulation and the2024BMFreference, never as invented2026quotations.

No actual-case pricing study or administrative software was run. Original main and v1.1 had zero executable worked examples; root added two explicitly fictional sourced examples in v1.2. The saved DE-L03 options predate that root edit. Both algorithms use declared hypothetical eligibility/comparability inputs, not invented statutory prices; no automatic CUP priority or median is implied. Derived internal controls separately test threshold inclusivity/lag, fixed-vs-proportional surcharge andAPAfees from the quoted own clauses, not as official worked-example quotations.
